<span class="line" data-line="4"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> neighbours</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(c):</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="10"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> path_from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(parent, goal):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    p </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [goal]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> parent[p[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">is</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> not</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="13"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        p.append(parent[p[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]])</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="14"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> p[::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="15"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="16"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> dfs_mark_on_push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(start, goal):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="17"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """和 agent.py 一样：放进栈的那一刻就写 parent，之后不再改"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="18"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    parent, stack, expanded </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {start: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}, [start], </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="19"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> stack:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        cur </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> stack.pop(); expanded </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cur </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> goal:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> path_from(parent, goal), expanded</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> neighbours(cur):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">not</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> parent:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">                parent[n] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cur</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">                stack.append(n)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> dfs_mark_on_pop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(start, goal):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """另一种写法：栈里放 (格子, 父)，出栈时才确认「访问过」"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="30"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    parent, stack, expanded </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {}, [(start, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)], </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="31" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> stack:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="32" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        cur, par </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> stack.pop()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="33" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cur </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> parent:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="34"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            continue</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="35"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        parent[cur] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> par; expanded </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1</span></span>
